# KUBICU — Design Decision Register

Labels: **Confirmed** · **Proposed** · **Illustrative** · **Needs validation** · **Pending founder**

Last updated: Batches 1–2 gap closure

## Sign-off log

- **27 Oct 2026 — Group A confirmed by the product owner (D233).** Every decision listed under Group A in `docs/DECISIONS_FOR_SIGNOFF.md` (money, limits and example figures) is **Confirmed** at the values shown there, including D40, D41, D47, D50, D59, D95, D99, D103, D112, D113, D114, D118, D119, D126, D127, D129, D131, D134, D135, D141, D144, D154, D156, D158, D183, D193, D197, D198, D200, D202, D224, D232 and the D61/D65/D222 figures. This log overrides the "Proposed" status shown in those rows for their figures.
- **Group B (legal wording):** rewritten to Draft 1.0 (D233); awaiting the owner's lawyer. Status stays Proposed until the lawyer signs off.
- **Group C (provider-dependent):** no action until providers are chosen. USSD payments switched off (D233).
- **Group D (translations):** deferred until further notice.
- **Group E (product and design):** no action needed.

| ID | Decision | Current position | Status | Notes / dependency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| D525 | Calmer Ask bar sweep after first use | Before the bar has been used, sweeps repeat every 5–12s (D523). After the first use in a visit, resumed sweeps (D524) repeat every 25–45s at random; the first resumed sweep still comes 1–3s after resuming. Resets on a full page reload. Figures in `AskBarGlow.tsx` (`GAP_FRESH`, `GAP_CALM`). | Proposed | Not checked in a browser. |
| D524 | Ask bar sweep resumes after engagement | Replaces D523's "stop for the session". Using the bar pauses the sweep; it resumes after 60s of the bar sitting idle and visible (not focused, empty, not dictating, not slid away or minimised — any of those restarts the count), and immediately when the person returns from Copilot or another page without the bar. No longer stored in session storage. | Proposed | Not checked in a browser. |
| D523 | Ask bar: colour sweep round the border until engaged | `components/shell/AskBarGlow.tsx`: a blue → purple → red → amber → green arc runs once round the floating Ask bar's border (2.4s, eased, fades in and out) from a random start angle, first after 1.2–3s, then every 5–12s at random. Stops once the person taps, clicks or focuses the bar (remembered for the browser session), while typing or dictating, while the bar is slid away or minimised, when the tab is hidden, and never runs with reduced motion. Ring is masked to 1.5px so the bar's surface and text contrast are untouched. Gradient used at the founder's explicit request. | Proposed | Not checked in a browser. |
| D522 | Copilot AI integration guide; handoff and docs refreshed | New `docs/COPILOT_AI_INTEGRATION.md` for the lead engineer: what stays (screens, `types/copilot.ts`, `makePlan` page building, limits, claiming, safety, knowledge, interface translations) and what's replaced (`respond` and its rule helpers; `understand.ts`, `copilotUnderstanding.ts` and keyword matching become unnecessary); target server architecture; the contract and context; tools with a link allowlist; grounding order (live settings → curated topics → Help centre guides → the person's words); language handling (model writes in the screen language, add `lang` to messages, keep stable English reply values); an 18-point behaviour list to keep as tests; safety, signed-in handling, limits/logging/staff switches, WhatsApp, testing and open decisions. `CLAUDE_HANDOFF.md` updated to D522 (new §18a, history D453–D522, open items, code map, flags and figures, checklist); `COPILOT_SPEC.md` (D519–D521 and the model guide), `AI_ASSISTANT_INTEGRATION.md`, `NOT_FOR_PRODUCTION.md`, `DECISIONS_FOR_SIGNOFF.md` and `DESIGN_GAP_AUDIT.md` refreshed. | Proposed | Docs only; no product change. |
| D521 | Copilot answers from every Help centre guide | When a clear question has no ready answer (D520 topics), Copilot looks for the best-matching Help centre guide (`findGuide` in `utils/copilot/askAnything.ts`: meaningful words from the question — plus English hints when typed in another language — scored against each guide's question (×3) and answer/where/topic (×1); needs a word in the guide's question and enough overlap, else it says it doesn't know). Replies with "Here’s what the Help centre guide says:" + the guide's short answer + "Read the full guide" → `/about/help/:id`, then carries on as in D520. New guides become answerable automatically. Guide text stays English on purpose; the lead line says so in other languages. Works at the start, mid-interview (clear questions only) and after the brief/draft. | Proposed | Word matching, not understanding — a real model is needed before launch. Not opened in a browser. |
| D520 | Copilot answers questions about KUBICU | Copilot is KUBICU's front door with building as its main job. A typed question about KUBICU gets a 1–3 sentence answer plus links to the screen where the person acts (`links` on `CopilotMessage`), then the conversation carries on: at the start it offers a page; mid-interview it repeats the current question ("Back to your page:"); after the brief/preview it keeps the last buttons. 17 topics in `data/copilotHelpTopics.ts` (what Copilot does + Assistant hand-off, payouts, verification, domains, short links/QR, bookings, products, orders/refunds, analytics, forms, WhatsApp, pricing, team, account/language, Business Coach hand-off, Help centre), matched in English plus the screen language (`utils/copilot/askAnything.ts`). Rules: asking for a page always builds; mid-interview only a clear question counts; an unknown question is answered only at the start, honestly, with the Help centre. Copilot never moves money, refunds or changes accounts from chat. Drafts in `data/translationsCopilotHelp.ts` (8 columns). | Proposed | Rule-based; a real model is needed before launch. Workspace links assume a signed-in person. Native speaker's check; not opened in a browser. |
| D519 | WhatsApp Copilot follows the chosen language | `/copilot/whatsapp` page, phone header, notice, list sheet, composer and the plan message now go through the translator; Copilot's messages use `translateCopilotText` like the browser chat; tapped answers translate, typed text shows as written. Drafts in `data/translationsCopilotWhatsApp.ts` (8 columns, merged into both dictionaries). Kiswahili/Twi/isiZulu notice says "national ID numbers" instead of BVN/NIN. Free-limit line uses "Label: n". | Proposed | Native speaker's check; not opened in a browser. Sample chat number stays +234. |
| D518 | Copilot understands typed answers in every language | `utils/copilot/understand.ts` reads the reply in the screen language: skip (only at the start of a reply), "you decide", "what do you mean?" (short replies only), looks, main action, offer, audience, photo/logo edits and "call it …" from `data/copilotUnderstanding.ts` (drafts, 7 languages), plus exact-word business/product terms from the search glossary (`englishForSentence`). Adds English hints for matching only; names, lists, numbers and places stay as typed. `/copilot` note changed to "Type your answers in your own language…". | Proposed | Word lists need a native speaker's check. Not opened in a browser. |
| D517 | Library-drop messages translated | `resolveLibraryDrop` takes the screen's translator; the drop target, refusal reasons, the full/locked/nesting messages and the "added" announcement now have drafts in all seven languages. Owner-typed block names stay as written. | Proposed | Not opened in a browser. |
| D516 | Canvas: library drops into containers; copy/paste buttons; smarter paste | Library drag now targets Section/Columns/Tabs (`resolveLibraryDrop` in `utils/canvasDrop.ts`, same fit/full/locked rules as moving), with an outlined target and message, red with a reason when refused. Block toolbar gains Copy and Paste (`hooks/useBlockClipboard.ts`, shared with ⌘C/⌘V), on desktop and touch. `insertBlocks(…, { smart })`: pastes inside the selected container when everything fits with room, otherwise at page level after the selection's container/group; groups only 2+ page-level non-container blocks. Touch keeps tap-to-add with select-first placement. Drafts in all seven languages. | Proposed | Not opened in a browser. |
| D515 | Canvas: drag from the library, and copy/paste blocks | **Drag:** desktop library cards and rows are draggable; dropping on the framed artboard adds the block between the nearest top-level blocks (a line shows where) and selects it. Click-to-add is unchanged; touch keeps click-only; containers aren't drop targets from the library. **Copy/paste:** ⌘C/Ctrl+C copies the selected block and its contents to a device clipboard (`utils/blockClipboard.ts`), ⌘V/Ctrl+V pastes fresh copies below the selection on any page or space, reusing the saved-sections insert (several blocks become a group). Text copying is left alone when words are selected. Drafts in all seven languages for these and for D514's booking settings and "Download CSV". `docs/CANVAS_INTERACTION_SPEC.md` updated. | Proposed | Not opened in a browser. |
| D514 | Leftover fixes, part 1 | **Ticket date:** "VIP goes on sale {date}" uses the screen language's month. **Twi and other languages a browser doesn't know:** "ago" times and short month names now fall back to drafted phrases (`relativeTime`/`ownMonthName` in `utils/localeFormat.ts`, translator passed by `LocaleProvider`) instead of English. **Phones:** the page assistant's free-text number sniff (`utils/assistant/engine.ts`) uses `findMobileNumber` instead of a Nigeria-only regex; the new booking-page dialog's WhatsApp placeholder uses `phonePlaceholder()`. Copilot and the claim form were already country-aware. **Bookings:** services that need approval can decline unanswered requests automatically after 12, 24 or 48 hours, or when their time arrives (`Service.autoDeclineHours`, `autoDeclineDue`, `BookingAutoDecliner` running every minute while KUBICU is open; deposit refunded in full, customer sees "Request declined"); Schedule has a **Download CSV** with one column per booking question. **DealRooms:** per-buyer referral links were already built (`ReferralShareCard`, `?ref=`); the D54 caveat is out of date. **Not done yet:** canvas drag from the block library and copy/paste between pages; drafts for the new booking strings. | Proposed | Not opened in a browser. |
| D513 | "2 hours ago" and month names follow the screen language | `formatRelative` (used on almost every screen — Home, lists, timelines, the staff console) was English in every language because it's a plain helper with no access to the translator. `LocaleProvider` now records the language it's rendering (`setActiveLang` in `utils/localeFormat.ts`); `formatRelative` uses the browser's own relative-time wording for that language ("saa 3 zilizopita", "3 hours ago" stays exactly as before in English), and `formatDate`/`formatTimestamp` use the language's month names while keeping the person's chosen date order and Western digits. Falls back to English where a browser has no data for a language (Twi often). Limit: the language is read during rendering, so a screen re-drawn on its own outside its provider's render could briefly use the last language drawn; with one language per screen this doesn't arise. | Proposed | Not opened in a browser. |
| D512 | Ticket and seat problems at checkout translated | The messages `ticketLineIssue` and `seatsIssue` build ("VIP sales have closed", "Only 3 VIP left", "Row B 4 was just taken — please choose another seat") already pass through the translator when checkout shows them; seven new patterns in `utils/builtPhrases.ts` now recognise them, so the ticket name and seat label are kept and the rest shows in the buyer's language. The fixed messages (no longer available, cancelled, ended, last seats held, just sold out, seat changed by the organiser) have drafts too. English wording is unchanged. "Goes on sale" keeps its English-formatted date. This finishes buyer-facing translation on sellers' pages. | Drafts — need native review. | Not opened in a browser. |
| D511 | Seat summary, Trust Passport evidence and the public passport page | `describeSeats` takes an optional translator and then reads "Row B · Seats: 4, 5" (English output unchanged without one); the seat picker and checkout pass it. `publicSignals` now also returns each public evidence line as a phrase with placeholders (`EvidencePhrase`), so the trust badge and the public Trust Passport show "Verified reviews: 6 · average 4.7", "Orders completed: 10+" etc. in the visitor's language; the tenure title has its own phrase ("Selling for {n} days"). The public Trust Passport page (`pages/PublicTrustPassport.tsx`) was entirely English and now follows the visitor's language, with dates via `dateLocale`. Not changed: seat errors at checkout ("Row B 4 was just taken…"), the owner's own Trust Passport screen and its share text. | Drafts — need native review. | Not opened in a browser. |
| D510 | Dates and lengths in the visitor's language | New `utils/localeFormat.ts`: `dateLocale(lang)` picks the closest locale the browser's date formatter supports (yo-NG, ha-NG, ig-NG, sw-KE, ak-GH, zu-ZA; falls back to en-NG / en-GB when a browser has no data, e.g. Twi often), and `durationText` writes "45 min", "1 h 30 min" through the translator. `formatEventDate`/`formatEventTime`, `dayLabel`/`timeLabel`/`formatInTz` and the Proof `monthYear`/`shortDay` take an optional locale; both `durationLabel`s take an optional translator. Every other caller keeps its old English output. Wired on buyer screens: Event block and the live (Commerce-linked) event card — which also had its own untranslated chips, ticket states, "On sale/Until {date}", "Left: n", repeat label, seat buttons and sold-out/paused lines, now translated — Countdown end date, Booking and Course blocks' lengths, Proof dates, the booking time picker (day chips, times, time-zone line, "Left: n of N", no-times message) and the booking sheet's header and confirm summary. Not changed: workspace and staff screens, the chosen-seats summary, Trust Passport evidence lines, and `utils/ops/slots` durationLabel's English "2 hours" wording when no translator is passed. | Drafts — need native review. | Not opened in a browser; how each browser formats Yorùbá/Hausa/Igbo/Twi dates varies. |

| D494 | Country rollout switch, country-aware kits, staff money in each workspace's currency | **Country rollout** (`utils/admin/countryRollout.ts`, `AdminStore.countries`): Super admin → Feature flags → **Countries** lists Nigeria, Kenya, Ghana and South Africa with Open/Closed, workspaces per country, an optional planned opening date, and Open…/Close…/Planned opening… actions (each asks for a reason and is audited). Nigeria is the launch market and can't be closed; missing = open. **Closed means:** sign-up now asks "Where is your business?" (first guess from the language) and a closed country shows "KUBICU isn't open for businesses in {country} yet" plus the planned date, and can't continue; the choice is saved (`kubicu.signup.country`) and onboarding creates the first workspace in that country and currency (`CreateWorkspaceInput.country`). Existing workspaces in a closed country open on a full-screen "KUBICU isn't open in {country} yet" (`CountryNotOpen`) with the planned date and links to the person's other open workspaces; published pages stay live for buyers and nothing is deleted. Settings → Country disables closed countries. **Kits:** `kitTemplateFor(kit, country)` starts the Fashion, Food, Home services, Digital creator, Music, Photography and Courses kits from the seller's own country's starter (D434) where one fits — onboarding, Templates → Business kits, applying a kit, and the Marketplace kit pages (visitor's language country); other kits keep their Nigerian page. **Staff money** (`utils/admin/workspaceMoney.ts`): Escrow cases, Payments & providers and Orders & disputes show each case, payout, wallet and order in its workspace's currency (amounts were already stored in it); the escrow refund box reads "Refund to buyer (KSh)" etc.; totals across workspaces show one figure per currency ("₦120,000 · KSh 8,500") instead of adding them; the ₦100,000 large-payout and ₦50,000 large-refund rules apply at the day's rate. Business reporting totals convert other currencies to naira at the day's rate and top sellers show their own currency. Drafted in all seven languages. | Proposed | Example figure: 48 hours. Not yet: evidence uploads, appeals. |

Worked out when the page is viewed, so it can't show a lost level; sample data never counts. Tapping opens the **public passport** at `/p/{handle}/trust` (checks met, not met yet, how it works, link to buyer safety tips; "a guide, not a guarantee" and a warning never to pay into a private personal account). Publish warnings when the app is off (badge hidden) or the level is "Just starting". `trust` is now a reserved page address. | Proposed | Independent partner checks (credit bureau, trade associations) still wait for a partner. |
| D112 | Pay on delivery and instalments (Batch 11 Pass C) | Both Pro+, run by the seller (no lender, no cash handling by KUBICU). **Pay on delivery:** delivery orders of physical items only (not pickup, downloads, tickets, DealRooms or made-to-order deposits); seller sets the largest order (default ₦100,000), zones, and cash / transfer / both; phones that refused before can be required to pay online. Order is placed with nothing charged (status New, payment pending, marked "Collect ₦X"), can be packed and booked with a rider; completing it requires recording "collected by cash / transfer" (or the rider's code handover), otherwise "Delivery failed" with a reason returns it to Packing. Transfers carry a warning about fake alerts. Cash never enters the wallet; the KUBICU fee is billed on the plan invoice. **Instalments:** per product 2–4 parts, weekly / every 2 weeks / monthly, send after the first or last part; offered on carts of ₦20,000+ where every item allows it (fewest parts, shortest gap, stricter shipping rule). Equal parts, first absorbs odd naira; explicit consent tick listing dates; card must confirm immediately. Later parts auto-charge while the app is open (outcome follows Simulate next renewal), retries days 1/3/5, then Defaulted. Buyer can pay a part early or update the card (a failed part is taken at once). Seller can retry, record as paid (cash, not wallet) or cancel remaining parts. Unpaid parts aren't counted as paid anywhere; each paid part reaches the wallet as a sale. Wallet refunds now also draw on balances/instalments paid later (closes the D95 gap). Opportunities, notifications and a Reports card. | Proposed | Example figures: ₦100,000 POD cap, ₦20,000 instalment minimum. No interest or fees; instalment wording needs legal review. Card-on-file charges simulated. |
| D113 | Seating maps and repeating events (Batch 11 Pass D) | **Seating (Pro+):** three starting layouts — Theatre rows, Banquet tables (8 × 8, guests can book a whole table, common for weddings, owambe and award dinners), Stage + standing (ticket types with no rows sell by quantity). Each row/table belongs to one ticket type and its quantity follows the map. Sold seats can't be removed, re-typed or the layout changed. Buyers choose seats in a Map or a List view (keyboard/screen-reader friendly), up to 10 seats per order and each type's max; exact seats are held 10 min and re-checked at payment so no seat sells twice. Seat shown on ticket, order page, guest list, CSV and check-in. **Repeating (all plans):** weekly / every 2 weeks / monthly, 2–12 dates; each date is its own event (own seats, guest list, check-in, cancel/refund) linked by a series; the Event block shows a date picker of upcoming dates; editing can copy shared details to later dates. Example limits: 30 rows, 40 seats per row. |
| D113a | Owner ticket view, seat map view, short addresses | Guest list rows open a view-only ticket sheet with a validity verdict (Valid · not used / Already checked in / Not valid / Not valid yet), seat, buyer, check-in time and a link to the guest's ticket page. Seated events get a Guest list / Seat map switch; the map shows checked-in, sold, held and free seats, and sold seats open their ticket. Typed short addresses (/health, /audit, /appeals and other staff areas) forward to /admin/…, which still requires staff sign-in and role; /availability, /services, /schedule, /events, /wallet and /account forward to their workspace screens. |

| D430 | Accessibility fixes from the code audit (items 9–14) | **Moving strips** on the home page (example pages, business types) get a visible pause/play button (`MarqueeToggle`, `data-paused` on the strip) so touch users can stop them; hidden under reduced motion, where they don't move (2.2.2). **Canvas panel toggles** use `aria-expanded` + `aria-controls` pointing at the new `canvas-left-panel` / `canvas-right-panel` ids; focus mode controls both (4.1.2). **Workspace navigation:** the phone bottom bar is "Workspace shortcuts", so it no longer shares the desktop sidebar's "Workspace" label (2.4.1). **SelectInput** accepts `required` and sets `aria-required` (callers opt in) (1.3.1). **Password checklist:** only the strength word is a live region, so the three rules aren't re-read on each keystroke (4.1.3). **Website footer:** one visually hidden "Site links" h2 with the column titles as h3s (1.3.1). New labels drafted in sw/tw/zu. Not yet: Pidgin, Yorùbá, Hausa and Igbo drafts for the four new labels. | Proposed |
| D429 | Accessibility fixes from the code audit (items 1–8) | From a code-review audit against WCAG 2.1 AA (no browser or screen-reader run). **Ask bar:** when it slides away on scroll it's also `inert`, so its controls leave the Tab order (2.4.3, 4.1.2). **Page language:** new `useDocumentLang` sets `<html lang>` from the screen language on the website and in the workspace (Twi as `ak`); published seller pages still set their own (3.1.1). **Checkout and booking errors:** themed field errors are `role="alert"` (4.1.3). **Delete on the canvas:** focus moves to the neighbouring block, or the canvas, when the deleted block had focus (2.4.3). **Command menu:** Tab stays on its search box; `aria-expanded` reflects results; `aria-autocomplete="list"` (2.4.3). **First error:** new `focusFirstError` (`utils/a11y.ts`) focuses the first `aria-invalid` field after a failed submit in sign-up, sign-in (password and single sign-on), checkout details and delivery, and booking details (3.3.1). **Checkout radio choices:** pickup times and delivery options get arrow/Home/End keys with choose-on-focus and a single Tab stop (`onRadioGroupKeyDown`, `radioTabIndex`) (4.1.2, 2.1.1). **Dialogs and sheets:** new `useScrollLock` (shared counter for stacked overlays) stops the page behind from scrolling (2.4.3). **Not yet done:** marquee pause button for touch (2.2.2), canvas panel toggles' `aria-controls`, two "Workspace" navigation landmarks, `aria-required` on selects, password checklist verbosity, footer heading level. | Proposed |
| D428 | Guest partners and legal wording follow the country | No answer to the two questions, so the recommended options were taken. **Guest partner bank step** (`pages/PartnerPortal.tsx`): the guest's country comes from their phone number's code; banks, account-number length (`accountNumberIssue`) and the ID check use that country's rules (`kycRules`) — Kenya national ID, Ghana Card, SA ID; Nigeria unchanged (BVN, 10-digit account). **Legal drafts:** Seller terms' verification consent (`data/legal/policyDocs.ts`) and the Privacy policy's verification-data line (`data/legal/legalDocs.ts`) now name each country's official sources, each tagged "[DRAFT — country wording pending legal review, D428]" so it can't be mistaken for approved text. The help answer on verification (`data/help/sell.ts`) no longer says "Nigerian regulations". **Still for a lawyer:** both legal clauses, and confirming each country's data-protection basis. | Proposed |
| D427 | Last Nigeria-only wording in money screens | **Staff risk flags** (`kycRisks`) name the case's own numbers and registry ("KRA PIN needs a closer look", "CIPC record shows inactive") using the stored case country, and a non-Nigerian case gets a flag "Applying from Kenya — check against that country’s rules" so reviewers know which rules apply. **Payments → Connect provider:** the registered-name placeholder reads "As it appears on your registration certificate" outside Nigeria (Nigeria keeps "CAC certificate"); drafts in sw/tw/zu. Left for a lawyer: the legal documents' verification consent (Terms, Privacy) still name BVN, NIN and CAC; the guest-partner bank step in the partner portal still uses BVN. | Proposed |
| D426 | Kiswahili, Twi, isiZulu — pass 55: country verification wording | New `data/translationsKeGhZa/kycCountries.ts` (≈150 rows) drafts every Kenyan, Ghanaian and South African verification string from `data/kycCountries.ts` (D424) — identity numbers and their hints and errors, ID types, official letters, registries, numbers, documents, people lists, tax numbers, business types and what each needs, "Not sure?" lines, consent wording, sales bands and description examples — plus the D425 wallet lines and starter checklist items. Wallet checks built as "{label} check" translate only for the four labels listed. Drafts need a native speaker's check; the consent lines need a lawyer. | Proposed |
| D425 | Verification follow-ups: starter payouts, limits and staff review | **Starter payouts** (`starterMissing`): the second number check is only required where the country has one, so Ghana and South Africa can reach Starter with one ID check (before, they could never unlock it because the NIN check never ran). Checklist items name the country's numbers ("National ID number check", "KRA PIN check"). **Limits** (`tierInfo`): still defined in naira, but shown and enforced in the workspace's currency at the example rates (D421) — before, a Kenyan wallet's ₦50,000 starter limit read as KSh 50,000. Tier descriptions name the country's numbers and registry. **Wallet** alerts outside Nigeria name the country's numbers and registry instead of BVN, NIN and CAC. **Staff review:** each case now stores its `country` (set when created; older submitted cases are Nigerian), and the case summary used in review shows that country's labels whatever country the staff console runs as. | Proposed |
| D424 | Seller verification follows the workspace's country | New `data/kycCountries.ts` holds per-country verification rules for NG, KE, GH and ZA; `kycRules()` in `utils/money/kyc.ts` picks them from the active country, and `entityInfo`, `stepLabels`, `volumeLabels`, `idTypeLabels` and `proofLabels` became getters so every existing caller follows the country without changes. **Nigeria** is unchanged (BVN, NIN, CAC RC/BN, FIRS TIN, NIBSS/NIMC). **Kenya:** national ID number (7–8 digits) and KRA PIN (A123456789B), IPRS/KRA checks, BRS registry (PVT-/BN- numbers), CR12, company KRA PIN, chief's letter as proof of address, KES sales bands. **Ghana:** Ghana Card number (GHA-123456789-0, also the individual tax number; no second number), NIA/GRA checks, ORC registry (CS…/BN… numbers), company profile and constitution, company TIN (C0012345678), assembly member's letter, Voter ID allowed, Ghana Card has an expiry, GHS bands. **South Africa:** 13-digit SA ID (no second number), Home Affairs/SARS checks, CIPC (2020/123456/07); the middle entity becomes **Close corporation** (CK number, founding statement CK1) and individuals are "Individual or sole proprietor"; disclosure certificate and MOI; 10-digit income tax number; ward councillor's letter; ZAR bands. Each country's ID types, expiring IDs, address example, business description example, "Not sure?" line and partner consent text follow too. Phone uses the country phone check (Nigeria keeps its 11-digit message). Simulated outcomes work the same way (digits ending 0000 fail, ending 9 need review; registry numbers ending 000 not found, ending 7 inactive). Storage is unchanged: the main ID number stays in `identity.bvn` / person `bvn`, the second in `identity.nin`, so saved cases load. **Open:** every format, registry and document name is an example to confirm with each country's licensed partner and a lawyer; payout limits (tiers) are still naira figures; the staff review sheet still shows Nigerian labels because the staff console runs as Nigeria; the new Kenyan, Ghanaian and South African wording is English in every language until the next translation pass. | Proposed |
| D423 | Typed amounts in website example pages follow the language | `localiseBlockAmounts` (`utils/previewMoney.ts`) runs every "₦…" found in block content text through `localiseAmounts` while the website's display conversion is on (D421). A no-op whenever the conversion is off (app, Copilot and assistant previews, published pages). Closes the gap noted in D421. | Proposed |
| D422 | Bricolage Grotesque restored | Reverses D417 and D418 at the owner's request: Bricolage Grotesque is a free Google font. `.kb-display` (website headlines) uses Bricolage Grotesque 800 again (-0.035em tracking); the "Grotesk" font pair and the `bricolage` Signature theme font use it again; the global import loads it instead of Plus Jakarta Sans. Inter is not used for display type. | Proposed |
| D421 | Website amounts follow the visitor's language | While the public website (`MarketingLayout`) is on screen in Kiswahili, Twi or isiZulu, it sets a display conversion (`setDisplayConversion` in `utils/currency.ts`): every naira amount formatted without an explicit currency — `formatNaira` (KUBICU's plan, template, theme and add-on prices) and default `formatMoney`/`formatNgn` (sample businesses in template previews, while the active currency is NGN) — is shown in KES, GHS or ZAR at the D373 example rates (₦12, ₦135, ₦85 per unit), rounded. Fixed text with "₦…" on Pricing (add-ons, provider fee, plan highlights, comparison cells and notes), the website FAQ list and Marketplace price labels goes through `localiseAmounts`. Pricing adds "Shown in {currency} at an example rate. The final price is confirmed when you pay." (drafts in sw/tw/zu). The conversion is cleared when the website unmounts; the app shell, published seller pages and staff console never convert, and a seller page in its own currency (active ≠ NGN) is never converted. Supersedes D373's "KUBICU's own prices stay in naira" for the website only — billing itself is unchanged. Not converted: amounts typed as plain words inside sample businesses' block text (e.g. "From ₦35,000" in a price note). | Proposed |
| D420 | Copilot and the page assistant accept numbers from all four countries | New `findMobileNumber(text, fallback)` in `utils/phone.ts` finds a complete mobile number anywhere in typed text and returns it in international form (+254712345678): numbers with a country code (+234/+254/+233/+27, 00…, or bare 254…) are read as that country; a local 0… number is read as the fallback country when the length fits, else as the only country whose length fits (11-digit 0… = Nigeria). Replaces the Nigeria-only regexes in `utils/copilot/engine.ts` (phone question), `utils/ai/brief.ts` (number in the opening message), `utils/assistant/engine.ts` (canvas page assistant, workspace country as fallback) and `ClaimNewAccount.tsx` (claim setup). Copilot has no workspace, so `useCopilot` sets the active country from the screen language via new `countryForLanguage` (sw→KE, tw→GH, zu→ZA, else NG); the claim form does the same. Numbers are now stored in international form, so wa.me links work for every country. New warning "That doesn’t look like a complete mobile number. Check the digits or add your country code (+234, +254, +233 or +27), or say skip." with drafts in all seven languages; the claim form error is English like the rest of that form. | Proposed |
| D419 | Kiswahili, Twi, isiZulu — pass 54: Copilot chat | New `data/translationsKeGhZa/copilotChat.ts` (mirrors `translationsCopilotWeb.ts` and the chat rows of `translationsCopilotChats.ts`, ≈150 rows) and `copilotQuestions.ts` (mirrors `translationsCopilotQuestions.ts`, ≈105 rows): chat chrome, All chats panel and sidebar, composer, privacy notice, progress, brief card, reply and look buttons, fixed messages, greeting, every question, help line and explanation, replies, change summary and ready message. Points to the live galleries at `/admin/design-system`. | Proposed | Code wins if they disagree. |

Lists 8 open layout decisions with recommendations (desktop detail pattern, 3-column wide galleries, desktop checkout dialog, phone plan picker, settings tabs on phones, tablet editor note, seller menu overflow, width tokens). | Proposed | Code wins if they disagree. |

Lists 8 open canvas design questions (artboard multi-select, block copy/paste, library drag, block render failure frame, block comments, zoom, density/container rename, phone Check panel). | Proposed | Code wins if they disagree. |

Handoff §5.3 now points to it; its visitor route list was corrected (most visitor pages live under `/p/:handle/`). | Proposed | Code wins if they disagree; update the doc in the same change. |
| D344 | Visitor form messages and searching in your language | **Forms:** every fixed message on sellers' forms (Forms engine, Lead form, Newsletter) and the website contact form follows the visitor's language — field errors (required, email, phone, numbers, name, date range, bank, country/state, ranking, grid), spam and daily-limit messages, Back / Next / Send, thank-you and preview lines, the ranking "This order is right" button. Messages with numbers ("Keep it under {n} characters") translate through the D341 patterns. The seller's own labels, help, consent and success wording stay as written. **Search:** a glossary of ~46 everyday words per language (`data/searchGlossary.ts`) lets block search (library and command palette) and help-centre search find English content from words typed in Naijá, Yorùbá, Hausa or Igbo; matching ignores tone marks and hooked letters. Help guides themselves stay English; the note now says you can search in your language. ≈55 form drafts + glossary per language. **Correction:** the seller page language switch was already the themed picker (D298, `VisitorLanguageMenu`); the earlier gap note was wrong. | Proposed | Glossary words and form drafts need a native speaker's check (Group D). |
| D343 | Website short-link clicks in Analytics; filling in older contacts | **Analytics:** short links that go to another website or a WhatsApp chat now count each real click as the visit in "Campaign links and QR codes" (and the Short links and QR screens that share the same numbers), because those visitors never open a page; bot and burst clicks stay out. A note under the table explains it. Headline visits stay page visits only. **Audience:** every contact shows "First came from" and "Permission for marketing". For contacts added before D268, the business can add the channel by hand and, only for people already marked subscribed, record what they agreed to and when. Hand-filled records are labelled "added by you" / "recorded by you" and logged in Activity; recording permission never turns marketing on. | D342 | Canvas gaps closed: FAQ assistant, Voice to content, copy checks, local search, rule badge | **AI FAQ assistant** is now a real block (`faqAssistant`, Smart): visitors type a question; it matches their words to the owner's approved questions (rule-based, `utils/faqMatch.ts`) and shows that answer word for word, with "Also asked" and "People often ask" chips; no match says so plainly and offers the owner's fallback link. Owners can copy answered questions from FAQ blocks. It never writes answers. **Voice to content** is a tool at the top of the block library (not a block): the browser's speech recognition (English, Yorùbá, Hausa, Igbo where supported; typing always works) fills an editable text box, then it becomes a paragraph, a heading + text, or questions and answers, previewed before adding (one undoable insert). KUBICU keeps no recording. **Composition** gains copy/hierarchy checks: competing openings, long page without headings, headlines over 12 words, all-caps headlines, paragraphs over 120 words, vague button words ("Click here", "Learn more"). **Block search** also matches each block's name, description and category in the screen language. **Rule badge** on canvas blocks and its tooltip now translate. The Planned shelf is empty. Drafts need a native speaker's check (D135). | Proposed | Matching and speech are prototype-level; a real AI provider could improve matching, never the answers. Speech support depends on the browser. |

Used only when the pattern has a draft, so English is untouched. Out of scope, unchanged: WhatsApp messages to customers, invoice lines, staff console. Drafts need a native speaker's check (D135). |

It starts a claim holding the draft's one-time code (`utils/copilot/claim.ts`, 30-minute window, example figure); the code is never in a URL or on screen, so a shared preview link can only look. **`/copilot/continue`** (sign-in look with the person's own draft on the lime panel, `noindex`): choose *I'm new* → sign up → confirm email (6-digit code) → setup prefilled from the chat (workspace name, your name, WhatsApp) → the draft becomes the new workspace's main page; or *I already have an account* → sign in → choose a workspace (switches to it; blocked with a reason if the role lacks `spaces.manage`, or "a new workspace instead") → the draft is added as a new **Page** space (new `SpaceKind 'page'`, not offered in New space). The step is saved, so an interrupted claim resumes. States: no claim, draft missing, link expired (30 min or `copilotFailure = expiredLink`), draft expired (7 days), code mismatch, already claimed (Open it in KUBICU if it's in one of this device's workspaces). `copilotFailure = signIn` makes the first code/sign-in attempt fail. Claiming spends the code; afterwards the chat won't edit the draft and points to the canvas assistant. **Canvas welcome** panel (once): it's a normal editable page, how to change the look, nothing is public, and the gaps still to fill in — `[Add …]` placeholders already block publishing. Copilot never publishes, connects payments or changes plans. | Proposed | Production needs real accounts, server-held single-use claim codes sent as signed short-lived links, and server-side transfer of the draft. Entry points, phone/accessibility checks and `docs/COPILOT_SPEC.md` are Pass D. |
| D263 | KUBICU Copilot — Pass B (WhatsApp channel, preview link, picture) | **`/copilot/whatsapp`**: a simulated WhatsApp chat (phone frame on desktop with a "what's real" column; full screen on phones under a "Simulated · proposed channel" strip) running the same engine as the web chat, keyed to a sample chat number used only for daily limits. Honours WhatsApp's limits: ≤3 reply buttons, otherwise a list sheet of ≤10 rows; brief sent as formatted text; draft sent as an image message (header picture + caption + buttons) followed by a link-preview message; no website inside the chat. Top notice says agent chats aren't end-to-end encrypted. **`/d/:id`** private preview: the page rendered exactly as visitors see it, one slim bar above (draft · not published · expiry · "N to fill in" list · copy link · back to chat), `noindex, nofollow`; expired (7 days or simulated) and missing states with return-to-chat / start-new. Expired drafts are never revived — changes after expiry make a new draft. **Picture** (`CopilotPagePicture`) = the PNG that a server would render; web chat shows it with the link and copy. Controls: `copilotWhatsApp = unavailable` shows the channel-unavailable screen with the web fallback and hides the WhatsApp button on `/copilot`; `copilotFailure = previewImage` makes the picture fail with "Send picture again"; `expiredLink` expires every preview link. | Proposed | Needs Meta approval for the agent channel, server-side PNG rendering, and unguessable server tokens for links. Claiming (Continue in KUBICU) is Pass C. |
| D262 | KUBICU Copilot — Pass A (conversation + web Copilot) | Signed-out, chat-first page creation with its **own conversation** (`utils/copilot/engine.ts`, words in `data/copilotQuestions.ts`), separate from the in-app assistant, but drafts are ordinary page documents built by the same starter/section/block rules (`makePlan` in `utils/assistant/layout.ts`). Opening "What are you creating today?" with 7 replies (WhatsApp list-safe) or free text; reads name/city/phone/goal/look/audience from what's typed; asks 3–6 of about · name · goal · look (real Signature themes on a sample page) · items (shop/food, prices only as typed) · photos · phone · city; "You decide"/"Skip" always work. Card numbers, passwords, PINs, BVN/NIN are removed, never stored. **Brief** card (creating, name, audience, goal, look, layout + reason, sections in order, gaps) with Create my draft · Change something · Add more details; typing after the brief or preview means a change. **Generation** shows 5 steps tied to real work (no percentages); failure keeps answers, doesn't count, offers Try again. Drafts saved on this device (`utils/copilot/store.ts`) with a one-time claim code, 7-day expiry, and daily limits of 3 drafts / 10 changes per browser or WhatsApp number (example figures); revisions update the same draft. Routes: `/copilot` (website entry, example starters `?start=`), `/copilot/chat` (chat + live phone preview, Chat/Preview tabs on phones, full-screen phone/desktop preview). New legal draft `/about/copilot`. Controls: `copilotWhatsApp`, `copilotFailure`. | Proposed | WhatsApp channel, `/d/:id` preview link and PNG (Pass B), claiming (Pass C), entry points and docs (Pass D) to follow. |
| D261 | Home hero on dark grey | The Home opening section changes from solid lime to a subtle, even dark-grey gradient (rgb 48→34→22, diagonal) at the owner's request; text turns white. This is an approved exception to D260's no-gradient rule, limited to the Home hero. The arch shape is now lime and the phone frame near-black so both stay visible. | Proposed |
| D260 | Website redesign — KUBICU Bold | Public pages (website, legal, sign-in, 404) move from Night market (D77) to a light, colour-blocked direction `kubicuBold`, using Linktree's structure and pacing as reference but with KUBICU's own palette, words and imagery (no copied branding or artwork). Cream canvas, full-bleed solid bands (lime/forest, cobalt, pink/plum, peach/clay, sky), heavy Bricolage Grotesque display type (`.kb-display`), floating white pill nav, dismissible announcement bar, plum footer with a white link card. Home is rebuilt as chapters: hero with link claim + photo/page collage; canvas; sharing; selling; analytics (sample data labelled); "Made for" photo cards + business-type marquee; Built-for-Nigeria bento; example-page marquee; status + FAQ; closing claim. Motion: word-rise headlines, scroll reveals, parallax photos, looping brand geometry (`KbShape`), marquees that pause on hover/focus; all honour reduced motion. The nm-* helpers are re-pointed to solid colours inside the new direction, so Pricing, Templates, legal and auth follow without gradients. People pictured are generated images of fictional owners. | Proposed |
| D259 | Settings for Audience, Automations and referral partners | Each opens from a **Settings** button in its page header as a side sheet (no new tabs). **Audience:** up to 8 of your own **contact fields** (Text, Number, Date, Pick one) shown on every contact and added to the CSV export (removing a field hides it; saved values are kept); up to 30 **saved tags**, offered as one-tap chips and suggestions when tagging, with "Already used" tags to adopt, plus a new **Tag** filter on the contact list; a **standard consent note**, offered in the canvas as "Use your standard consent note" under the consent field of enquiry-form and newsletter blocks — it never changes published pages on its own. **Automations:** **quiet hours** (off by default, 21:00–07:00, business time zone, crossing midnight handled) — customer WhatsApp/email steps written then are logged as held until the end time; tags, follow-ups and notices to you aren't held; and a **default channel** (WhatsApp or Email) that new "Action" steps start with (recipes keep theirs). **Collaborations → Referral partners:** starting commission % (1–50, default 10), starting terms (default sentence built from rate and timing), and **when commission becomes payable** (as soon as paid, or 3/7/14/30 days after payment; default 7, was a fixed 7) — this one applies to every partner's not-yet-payable commission; existing partners keep their agreed rate and terms. Settings are stored with each area's data (`OpsData.settings`, `CollabData.settings`). | Proposed |
| D258 | Analytics: devices, and new vs returning visits | Every recorded event now carries the **kind of device** — phone, tablet or computer — worked out from the browser's own description (iPads that report as Macs count as tablets). Nothing else about the device is kept. Analytics gets a two-column row under "Where visitors come from": **Devices** and **New and returning**, each with share of visits, a bar, the count and conversions. A visit is **returning** when the same anonymous browser id had an earlier visit anywhere in the kept history (not just the period on screen), so it's an estimate like Visitors. Visits from before this show as "Not recorded"; sample visits get a device (about 72% phone, 22% computer, 6% tablet). CSV export gains a `device` column. The visitor notice in Settings → Privacy now says device kind is recorded and explains the browser id honestly (it outlasts the 30-minute visit; clearing the browser removes it). | Proposed |
| D257 | Quotes & jobs settings | New **Settings** tab in Quotes & jobs (one section with its own Save, same pattern as Commerce settings). **Numbering:** quote and invoice prefixes (letters, numbers, - and /, up to 8; default Q- and INV-; must differ), with "Next quote: Q-0003" previews; counters don't reset and existing documents keep their numbers. **Quotes stay valid for** 7/14/30/60 days (default 14). **Invoices are due** on receipt or 3/7/14/30 days (default 7), also used for payment-stage invoices unless the stage has its own date. **Starting note** on every new quote, and **payment terms** on every new invoice (new `Invoice.note`, editable on drafts, shown on the pay page and printed invoice). **VAT:** "Charge VAT on quotes and invoices" uses the rate and inclusive/added-on-top choice from Commerce settings → Tax — one rate for the whole business. The rate is copied onto each quote when it's created (`Quote.vat`) and onto its invoices, so later changes never alter a sent document; it can be switched off on a single draft. Totals, balances, "paid in full", lists, the WhatsApp share text, the client quote page, the pay page and the print all include VAT; deposits and stages are worked out before VAT and each invoice adds it ("+ VAT"). Settings apply to new documents only. Not included: VAT collected from quotes in the Sales report and accounting export (Commerce VAT only), per-line VAT rates. Also: the printed invoice now names the workspace's currency instead of always "Nigerian naira". | Proposed |
| D256 | Bookings: approve each booking yourself, and your own booking questions | **Approval** is per service (Services → Before they book → "Approve each booking yourself", off by default). New bookings for that service get status `requested`: the time is held (slots, staff load, class seats), any deposit is taken, no meeting link yet, and "Confirm every booking"-style automations wait until it's accepted. The sheet says "Send your booking request" and explains the deposit is refunded in full if declined. **Seller:** Schedule shows "N requests to answer" above the list (ignores filters); booking details have Accept booking / Decline (reason optional, deposit refunded in full, time freed); the Schedule tab, the Bookings sidebar item and the bell (new event "Booking request to accept or decline", urgent defaults) count open requests. Requests whose time passes unanswered can only be declined. **Customer:** status page says "Request sent" / "Request declined" (with the reason and refund), and they can withdraw a request with a full refund. **Questions:** up to 5 per service, each Short answer / Pick one (2–6 options) / Yes or no, required or optional, reorderable; asked after name and number in the booking sheet, validated, stored on the booking with the question's wording copied, and shown in booking details above "They told you". The free "Anything we should know?" box stays. Not built: auto-decline after a deadline, answers in the CSV export. | Proposed |
| D255 | Group 1 fixes: stock holds, Delivery & returns on phones, order prefix everywhere, settings links, canvas tabs | **Stock holds:** Commerce settings → Orders & stock → "Keep stock for buyers during checkout" (default on; off and disabled while backorders are on). Pressing Checkout keeps stock-tracked physical items (not pre-orders or gift cards) for 15 minutes (example figure) under the same per-tab holder id as seat holds (stored in `ticketHolds` with `kind: 'stock'`). Other buyers' holds count as taken in the cart ("The last X is in someone else's checkout — try again in a few minutes") and again at payment in `placeOrder`; the buyer's own hold counts as theirs. Timer, one extension and release-on-expiry reuse the seat-hold UI ("Your items are kept for you"). Paying or closing checkout releases holds immediately. Product cards don't yet reflect others' holds — the cart does. **Delivery & returns block:** two columns only when the block itself is ≥640px wide; the "Usually" column only at ≥380px (otherwise the time sits under the area); prices never wrap. **Order prefix:** activity log, toasts, comments, notifications, opportunities and nudges, wallet ledger, instalments, balances, pop-up slips and toasts, sales report, guest list CSV, proof sources, watermark text, live automation events and the courier sheet use `orderRef`. Admin cross-workspace screens still show `#number`. **Settings links:** Commerce settings now scrolls to a `#section` on arrival; Storefront "Delivery options", Delivery block "Edit in Commerce → Settings" and Commerce Overview delivery links go to `#delivery`, "Edit in Policies" to `#policies` (previously landed at the top), Opportunities "Checkout settings" to `#checkout`. **Canvas tabs:** dense tabs with no visible scrollbar; the Check count is a badge; the Pages count was dropped from the tab label. **Follow-up:** the shop's product page counts other buyers' holds — "Only N left — others are in buyers' checkouts", or, when all are held, "The last ones are in other buyers' checkouts. If they don't pay, they're back here within 15 minutes." Product cards and grids are unchanged (they'd flicker between Sold out and In stock as holds come and go). | Proposed |

**Pay page:**

- each payment shows email, address and answers;

| D211d | AI page assistant — Pass D: changes by chat, removal, credits and failures | **Opening:** the assistant leads with a tap-to-answer question (business type chips + "Something else"); typed answers are read for type, name, area, WhatsApp, offer, action and price lists. Name offers the workspace name as one tap; location offers city chips and "Online only". **After the build** the chat stays open: suggestion chips (warmer, bolder, add gallery/FAQ/hours) or typed requests; tapping a section in the preview focuses it ("Move this up", "Remove this", "Add a call to action after this"). Each request becomes a **proposal** (`utils/assistant/refine.ts`, rule-based, returns a whole valid page — never code) shown with a **Before / After** switch and the affected section outlined; **Keep** applies it as one undoable step, **Discard** drops it, the latest kept change can be **Undone** from the chat. Supported: look changes, add a section (gallery fills with the conversation's photos; FAQ/hours/location/team/policies/delivery come with gaps), remove, move, and a new headline only in the owner's exact words. **Declined, no credit used:** writing reviews/testimonials (offers Review summary of real reviews instead), making up prices, adding guarantees/awards/qualifications, rewriting copy (prototype), unknown requests (lists what's possible). **Costs:** 1 credit per proposal, 5 per build. **Out of credits:** a card explains nothing was charged, with "Get more credits" and "Edit in the canvas"; the plan is kept. **Failure / offline:** "Nothing was changed and no credit was used" with Try again. **Remove everything** (replaces "Undo the build"): confirmation lists what goes — the page returns to its pre-build snapshot (including later kept changes), created products without orders, services without bookings, the QR code and link, and the saved layout; items with orders/bookings are kept and named; photos stay in Media; credits aren't returned. | Proposed | Real model must return the same proposal shape and obey the same refusals. |

Prices come only from what the owner typed (`utils/assistant/priceList.ts`: "15,000", "₦8.5k", "N4500"; a number under ₦50 stays part of the name). **During the build**, right before the products or services section, unpriced items trigger an inline question ("Asun 4500" or a bare number when only one is missing); the build waits, answers re-plan just that step, Skip leaves them as drafts. **Photos:** first → main image, next ones matched to products in order (assumption shown, changeable in Commerce), the rest → gallery. **Created on approval:** Commerce products with the list's ids (priced → active, unpriced → draft, stock counting off, matched photo), linked from the menu block (menu items carry `productId`) or a catalogue product grid (WhatsApp ordering, or checkout when the main action is "Buy online"); Bookings services (60 min, location note = city) linked from pricing packages and the booking block when Bookings is installed — otherwise packages say "Enquire" and booking blocks become an enquiry form; a QR code + campaign link (`{name}-qr`, source QR, to this space). | D211 | AI page assistant (Passes A–B) | Full-screen `/app/assistant` replaces the D63 AI page setup dialog and the onboarding AI kit dialog. Chat on the left (messages, quick-reply chips, free text, photo upload, typing state, read aloud via a polite live region), live preview on the right (real block renderers at 390 px phone or 1,180 px desktop, scaled), Chat/Preview tabs on phones. **Flow:** brief → questions one at a time, only for what the brief didn't settle (business, name, audience, main action, offer, city and WhatsApp when relevant, feel shown as theme swatches, logo, photos, and replace-or-add when the page has content) — always at least 3 → **plan card** (template used or "composing a new layout", sections in order, what it creates, gaps, assumptions, "Prefer something else?") → Approve (5 AI credits) / Change something (look, main action, name, number, or "compose from scratch") / Start over → live build one section at a time (150 ms; instant with reduced motion) with Pause → applied as one undoable change (`replaceBlocks`, or `insertBlocks` for "add") → finish card with gaps and Fix, Open in canvas, Keep as draft, Undo. **Layout:** starters (free ones plus premium ones the workspace owns) are scored on business match, brief keywords, main action, offer and feel; ≥9 uses the starter via the D63 draft rules, otherwise a page is composed: brand header (if logo) → hero (variant from offer/feel) → main action → offer section (ready-made sections/blocks) → gallery → About in the owner's words → location & hours when relevant → FAQ (answers left blank) → contact. Feel → a Signature theme on Creator+, a Classic theme on Free. Composed layouts are saved privately in the library (`templates`) and listed under "Your templates" in the canvas Templates panel. Logos go to Media → Brand assets, photos to Product images (products/menu) or unfiled. Sessions are saved per page on this device and resume. Entry points: onboarding Starter step ("Build it with the assistant" opens it after setup), Overview, My Spaces, canvas page panel. | D198 | Group classes in Bookings | A service can be a **group class** with 2–50 seats per time (example limit). A class time stays bookable until its seats are gone; visitors see "6 of 10 left" on each time. Everyone in one class counts as one appointment towards the daily limit, and a class blocks other bookings at that time. Classes are booked for the business as a whole, not a team member. Schedule → Upcoming shows "Coming classes" with seats taken, who's coming (each opens their booking) and **Cancel class**, which cancels every attendee as the owner, so every deposit is refunded. Seats can't be set below a coming class's head count, and a service with booked classes can't be switched back to one-to-one. | Proposed | Live video isn't inside KUBICU — online classes use your own meeting link in the location note. Messages are previews only. |

Access: **Free** (Creator+; sign in with a one-time code to enrol), **Paid** (Pro+; sold through a hidden companion product of kind `course`, so checkout, discounts, orders and refunds work as usual — a refund or cancellation ends access, pending payments wait), or **With a membership** (active/trial subscribers of chosen plans; paused or ended memberships lock lessons, progress is kept). Learners reuse the members' one-time-code sign-in on that workspace. Public course page `/p/{handle}/learn/{id}`: overview, outcomes, outline with done/preview/locked states in words, lesson player with Mark as done, Previous/Next, "Continue where you left off", and certificate. Seller: list with learners, average progress and sales; Learners sheet with progress, CSV, add by hand (e.g. paid by transfer) and remove. Course block on pages (Card or Card + modules). Order confirmation shows "Start learning"; course products don't count towards the product limit and can also appear in the shop. | Proposed | Example figures: limits, Creator/Pro gates. Simulated: sign-in codes, file storage; lessons are hidden only in the browser. Videos must be set to Unlisted on the host. |

Features in one list grouped Finding / Buying / Trust: existing switches unchanged, plus saved items and recently viewed (kept on the visitor's phone only), related products and sale badges (previously always on), Ask on WhatsApp (checkout shops), Share, and "What customers said" (Proof reviews matched to completed orders containing the product; renders nothing with zero reviews). Add/+− from the listing adds without opening the cart; options open the existing chooser; WhatsApp shops get an Order button instead. | D195 | Forms connected to the rest of KUBICU | (1) Automations: new "A form response arrives" trigger, any form or one chosen form, fires per real response even without contact details; "Someone becomes a lead" keeps covering Lead/Newsletter blocks and forms with contact details. Needs the Forms add-on. (2) Confirmation email per form (Settings → Confirmation email): subject + message with {first_name}/{name}/{form}/{answers}; {answers} never includes bank details, signatures, files or payments; sent through the active email provider in Integrations (simulated, logged in Activity); outcome shown on the response (Sent / Not sent — no provider / no address). (3) Inbox "Connected" panel: open the contact in Audience; "Create order" turns product picks into a real Commerce order (choose options, pickup or delivery, stock reserved; marked paid only if the form payment covers the total, otherwise waits for payment); "Book this time" creates a real booking from a Booking time answer if the slot is still free (no deposit taken). Print check gains: QR printed size in mm (fails under 15 mm, warns under 18 mm), big or small text on small pieces, photo sharpness accounts for zoom, stamp card needs a reward, label without a phone. **Actual size view**: full screen, one piece or the whole A4 sheet (front/back for cards), true millimetres after a one-time "Match my screen" check against a bank card (saved per device). New print-shop prices and finishes for stamp cards and labels are example figures. | Proposed | Older saved designs pick up the new defaults. |

Locks are editor-only and don't count as unpublished changes. | **Confirmed** for first release | Founder confirmed. |
| D22 | Block grouping | Group two or more blocks from Layers → Group. A group is one contiguous run: it drags, moves, locks, hides, duplicates and deletes as a unit; it can be renamed and collapsed. Members reorder inside the group in Layers. Dragging a member out on the canvas removes it from the group; groups with fewer than two blocks dissolve. Groups are editor-only. | **Confirmed** for first release | Founder confirmed. Nested groups not supported (Proposed). |
| D23 | Appearance governance | **Super admin → Design system** decides which appearances are available and which is the default (default is always available; at least one must stay on; withdrawing asks for confirmation and is logged). **Workspace users** pick from available ones under "Appearance" (sidebar footer / More on mobile), remembered per user; if withdrawn, they fall back to the default and their choice returns if re-enabled. Public pages are unaffected — they use the page theme. | **Confirmed** | Founder confirmed. Per-tenant overrides: Proposed for later. |

The menu shows home + pages not hidden from it. The site publishes and versions as one unit. Changing a live page’s address redirects the old one; deleting a live page redirects it to home. Each space has its own link, theme, blocks, versions and publishing; all share the workspace’s products, stock, orders, bookings, contacts, automations, media and (later) discounts. Campaigns and DealRooms are one page; only the main space grows into a multi-page site. Archiving forwards the link to the main page and keeps orders and contacts. Allowance of extra spaces: Free 0 · Creator 3 · Pro 15 (example figures); over the allowance a space stays editable but can’t publish — nothing is deleted. Workspace screens always describe the main space; other spaces open in the canvas. Replaces D15. | Proposed | Per-space analytics and custom domains come in Batch 5. |
| D51 | Discounts | Three kinds: codes, automatic offers, referral codes. One discount per order; when several apply the customer gets whichever saves more and is told why. Discounts reduce what the customer pays before fees are calculated; refunds return the amount actually paid. Uses are counted from placed, non-cancelled orders; per-customer limits match phone numbers. A discount with orders can be paused but not deleted. Referral rewards are notes the seller honours — KUBICU pays nothing out. | Proposed | Discounts apply at KUBICU checkout (Pro); WhatsApp orders don’t apply them automatically. |
| D52 | Campaign pages | A campaign is a one-page space with a window (start optional, end required) and three phases — Before, Live, Ended — derived from the clock, never set by hand. Blocks choose the phases they show in (default: all). The link never breaks: after the end it shows the Ended version until archived (then forwards to the main page). A linked discount code applies automatically at checkout during Live only, and is still re-checked against its own rules. Orders record the link they came from for campaign results. | Proposed | Sign-up counts per campaign and scheduled “it’s live” messages wait for Analytics and real messaging. |
| D53 | Storefront | An auto-built shop at `/shop` on the workspace’s main page (and its other pages), listing every active product with collection tabs, search, sort and an in-stock filter, plus a page per product (`/shop/{id}`) with all photos, options with per-option stock, and one clear buy action. It reads Commerce live — no publishing. Ordering is cart + checkout on Pro, WhatsApp otherwise. Campaign spaces never show it. | D54 | DealRooms | One product on its own link, chosen per room as a **limited drop** (a set number of units at a room price, until the deadline or sold out; capped by real stock) or a **group goal** (buyers pay now; orders are held and not fulfilled until the goal is reached; if it’s missed at the deadline every held order is refunded in full automatically and stock returns). Counters only ever use real paid orders — no seeded or padded numbers. Plain terms sit above the buy button; per-person limits match phone numbers. Once anyone has joined, terms lock; the seller may extend the deadline (reason recorded, buyers told) or end early. Updates to buyers are previews. DealRooms need checkout (Pro). Settlement runs in the browser while KUBICU is open; in production it runs on the server at the deadline. | Proposed | Refund fees on a missed goal (D52 caveat) need a payment-provider check. Personal referral links per buyer are not built yet. |

KUBICU returns its fee on full refunds and waives it on group-goal orders refunded because the goal was missed. The seller sees top referrers and sets an optional reward note they honour by hand — nothing is paid out automatically. | Proposed | Referral counts only include paid, non-cancelled orders. |
| D57 | Roles and permission table | Five roles — Owner, Admin, Editor, Sales, Viewer — defined by one role × action table (`data/permissions.ts`) shown in Settings → Roles. Anything a role can’t do stays visible but disabled with the reason (“Only Owners and Admins can …”); screens you can only view show a “View only” line; the canvas shows a read-only door with Preview instead of the editor. Editors and Sales can be limited to chosen spaces (Owner/Admin always have every space). Sales can refund up to a per-workspace limit (default ₦50,000). Visitors’ checkouts, bookings and sign-ups, and automatic DealRoom settlement, are never limited by the signed-in member’s role. | Proposed | Prototype enforces in the browser; the server must enforce the same table. |
| D58 | Approvals | Off by default; an Owner or Admin switches it on in Settings → Approvals. When on, everyone without “Approve changes” (Editors) gets **Send for review** instead of Publish/Schedule, with their note and optional go-live time. One open request per space; sending again replaces it, and later edits update the request automatically (marked “updated since”). Reviewers see live vs proposed side by side, the list of added/changed/removed blocks and setting changes, then **Approve & publish** (or schedule) — which publishes exactly the reviewed version — or **Request changes** with a required comment. If the request changes while a reviewer has it open, approving waits until they confirm they’ve seen the latest. Requesters can withdraw. Reviewers get a sidebar badge, an Approvals tab count and a Needs-attention chip; requesters get in-app notices (Overview chip, toast in the canvas) and an “In review” / “Changes requested” badge in the canvas. | Proposed | Notices are in-app only; email/WhatsApp notifications come with real messaging. |

At the limit Invite is locked with an upgrade button. Downgrading never removes anyone: members beyond the seats (latest joiners first) become paused Viewers until there’s room, and the Team screen says who. Invites last 7 days; one pending invite per address. | Proposed | |
| D60 | Assignments, activity and presence | Orders, bookings, contacts (“Looked after by”) and follow-up tasks can be assigned to any team member; lists show who and offer a **Mine** filter (contacts count as mine if I look after them or have an open follow-up). Removing a member moves their **open** work to the person removing them; finished work keeps their name. Automations gain “Notify a team member”, and “Create a follow-up” can be assigned. Settings → Activity lists who did what (publishing, scheduling, reviews, refunds, order steps, assignments, team and settings changes), grouped by day, filterable by person, area and period, with CSV export and links to the thing changed. | D61 | Analytics and privacy | Public pages record views, taps (per block and label), product views, cart adds, checkout starts, paid orders (with value), booking opened / time chosen / confirmed, form sends and sign-ups — never in draft previews or for example pages. A visit is a random 30-minute session id; unique visitors are estimated from a random browser id; no name, email, phone or cross-site tracking. Source comes from `?src=` / `utm_source` / campaign link first, then the referring site (WhatsApp, Instagram, TikTok, Facebook, X, Google, QR, Other, Direct). Data is kept 90 days (example) and can be switched off or deleted in Settings → Privacy; “don’t count my own visits” is off by default in the prototype because owner and visitor share one device. The demo workspace has 30 days of labelled sample visits. Analytics shows its period, definitions and sample size; Link Intelligence needs 30+ visits, shows its evidence and never claims cause. “See analytics” is a permission for Owner, Admin, Editor and Sales. | Proposed | Notice wording pending legal review. |
| D62 | Domains, QR codes, campaign links and redirects | **Domains & QR** module (`/app/domains`) with Domains · QR codes · Campaign links · Redirects. **Custom domains** (Creator and above — example gating; upgrade button where locked): two-step wizard (domain → whole domain or subdomain → which space; then DNS records with copy buttons and illustrative registrar tips) and a status card that moves *Waiting for DNS → Verifying → Certificate → Live* or shows a *Problem* with a troubleshooting checklist, or “can’t see records yet” after a timeout — never Live before verification and certificate. Checks are simulated with a “what the check finds” control; record targets are examples. Once live: primary address (one per space), “send kub.icu visitors here”, disconnect with consequences; product links become readable (`/shop/ankara-wrap-dress`, resolving the D53 note). **Campaign links** (every plan): `kub.icu/{main handle}/s/{slug}` with a name, source and destination (any space home, page, shop or product); address fixed once created, destination editable; visits, orders and revenue per link from Analytics; archive shows visitors “this link has ended”. **QR codes** (every plan): each encodes a campaign link (source QR) so scans are counted; colour presets and pickers with a contrast check (downloads off below 3:1, warning for inverted or low contrast), optional initials in the middle (high error correction), caption, PNG/SVG download and print sheets (A6 card, A4 poster, table tent). **Redirects**: your own (from a path in any space to a page or an https website; reserved words and existing pages refused) plus the automatic ones (renamed/removed pages, archived spaces). | D77 | Night-market website look | The KUBICU website, legal, trust and sign-in pages use a dedicated dark "Night market" direction (ink #100E1A, off-white #F3EEE6) with the brand gradient orange #FF8A3D → pink #FF3D8B → electric blue #3D7BFF (`.nm-fill`, `.nm-text`, `.nm-frame`, `.nm-edge`, `.nm-rule`, Button `brand`). | D95 | Milestone payments in Quotes & jobs | A quote is paid either "Deposit + balance" (unchanged) or "In stages": 2–6 seller-named stages as whole percentages adding to exactly 100% (last stage absorbs rounding). Each stage is due when the client accepts, when a named job checklist step is ticked (the step is added to the job automatically), on a date, or when the job is done. Four built-in templates (Bridal 30/20/50, Half/half, Thirds, Materials then labour) plus the seller's own saved templates. Accepting a staged quote auto-requests stages due on acceptance; ticking a step prompts "Request payment". Each requested stage is an invoice already marked sent, with its own client pay link (`/p/:handle/invoice/:id`) showing the whole payment plan; any sent invoice can now be paid online (test card). Online payments follow the Quotes & jobs payment setting (direct = no wallet entry; wallet = settles 24h; escrow = held 2 days) and appear in the wallet as "Milestone". Bookings (round 2): a service can "Ask for the rest online before the appointment" (due 2/12/24/48/72 hours before). The deal is copied onto each booking; the customer pays the balance from their booking page (test card) and it follows the Bookings payment setting; the seller sees balance status, copies the pay link or records it as paid; cancelling refunds a paid balance in full. Opportunities lists due stages and due/overdue balances; Notifications adds "Payment stage or booking balance due". Round 3: physical products can be "Made to order" (deposit 20–80% at checkout, typical lead days). Checkout charges only the deposit (shown as "Deposit to pay now" + "Balance before dispatch"); fees are taken on what is charged; the order carries `balance`, and its "customer paid" figure excludes the unpaid balance. The buyer pays the balance from their order page (test card; follows the Shop payment setting); the seller can request it (copy link / WhatsApp) or record it as paid, and the order can't be marked out for delivery or completed until it's in. Not used inside DealRooms. Fixed-amount payment links can be "Paid in stages" (2–6 named stages adding to 100%, built-in splits as quick starts): one payer, in order, link closes when every stage is paid, stages lock after the first payment, the pay page shows the whole plan. Automations gain the "A payment stage or balance is due" trigger (test runs only, like the others). Reports adds Staged payments (still owed, balances collected, link stages paid). Opportunities lists made-to-order balances. Status: Proposed. Known gap: cancelling or refunding a booking/order balance paid into the wallet doesn't yet debit the wallet. |

Super admin → **Escrow cases** (Trust & safety, Finance, Platform lead) shows both sides and decides release / full refund / split with a note shown word for word to both, audited. Saved as you type; Owner only. **Payouts (manual and scheduled) are blocked until a KUBICU reviewer approves** — sales still arrive. Approval sets limits: individual ₦2m/day, ₦10m balance; registered business ₦20m/day, no cap. The old instant "Tier 1" is retired; pre-D93 wallets restart verification. Simulated: documents aren't uploaded (name/size only); numbers ending 0000 fail and ending 9 need review; CAC ending 000 isn't found, ending 7 is inactive. **Example figures:** limits, 24-hour review target, pre-verification balance cap ₦300k.
