Staff & access), **Trust & safety**, **Support**, **Finance**, **Engineering** — filter the nav and block other areas with a “not part of your role” screen. **Staff & access** (leads only): invite, change role, reset two-step, disable/enable, cancel invite, each with a reason; you can’t change your own access and at least one active lead must remain. Every audit entry now names the signed-in staff member and role; customers see only a first name on replies. **Appeals**: owners appeal a takedown or suspension from the danger banner in their app (statement ≥ 30 chars); staff review in Super admin → Appeals with the original reason, decider and linked reports; the person who made the original decision can’t decide it. Reverse restores the page/workspace immediately; uphold keeps it. The owner sees the note word for word. | Proposed | Simulated identity (any 8+ char password, any 6 digits except 000000); no backup codes or dual approval for high-risk actions yet. |
| D82 | Super admin: Pages & domains, Orders & disputes, Business reporting, Data requests | **Pages & domains** lists every space link (main, campaign, DealRoom) with Live/Draft/Taken down/Workspace suspended and open report counts; take down and restore reuse the moderation takedown and need a reason. Domains show status with "Stuck over 48 h" and the last check problem (read-only — owners fix DNS). **Orders & disputes** queues open disputes (seller response deadline), refunds ≥ ₦50,000 in 30 days and payments pending > 24 h; staff can add private notes and **Message the seller**, which opens a support request in the seller's Help → My requests. Staff never refund or move money. **Business reporting** shows MRR (list price, yearly ÷ 12, trials ₦0), amount sold, KUBICU fees, new workspaces, visits, plan mix and top sellers for 7/30/90 days, each with a written definition. **Data requests** handles see/correct/delete requests with a 30-day deadline (example), a per-kind checklist, and complete/refuse with an outcome note; account self-service exports and deletions are listed read-only. All actions go to the audit log (new areas: Pages, Orders, Data requests). | Proposed | Real version reads the platform database; data-request deadline and process need legal sign-off. |
| D81 | Opportunity Inbox + visitor preferences (Batch 8 start) | **Opportunities** (sidebar, Business group) lists only real records: unpaid checkouts older than 1 h (up to 14 days), subscriptions in a failed-payment grace period, future bookings with an unpaid deposit, no-shows in the last 30 days, form leads unanswered after 24 h, overdue follow-ups, active sold-out products and automations with failed runs this week. The lead figure is the sum of known amounts only; leads and follow-ups show "No amount" rather than an estimate. Each row has one next step — **Message** opens WhatsApp with an editable suggested note (never sent automatically) or **Open** — plus Mark as handled and snooze (tomorrow / next week), with Undo and a Hidden view to bring items back. State is per workspace on this device. **Preferences page** `/p/:handle/preferences/:contactId` (copy from a contact's Consent section) lets the person unsubscribe (optional reason) or opt back in themselves; owners still can't re-subscribe anyone (D45). Both actions are logged on the contact's timeline. | Proposed | Real abandoned carts need checkout-session capture with consent; preference links need adding to sent messages once messaging exists. |
| D80 | Personal account, separate from workspaces | **/app/account** (sidebar account menu at the bottom; phone "More" sheet) holds everything about the person, the same in every workspace: **Profile** (photo cropped square, full and display name, job title, about; email change by link with current password and a pending state; phone add/verify by 6-digit code), **Sign-in & security** (password change with strength meter and "sign out other devices"; two-step sign-in by authenticator app with QR + key or by text message once a phone is verified, then 10 one-time backup codes that must be copied or downloaded; regenerate codes; sign-in alerts; devices with sign out one/all others; recent sign-ins incl. failed attempts; Google/Apple logins), **Preferences** (language — English now, five more "coming soon"; time zone; date format with live preview; text size Standard/Larger and motion Follow device/Always reduce applied app-wide; start screen used after sign-in; links to per-workspace notifications and appearance), **Workspaces** (all workspaces with role, plan and members; open; owners can't leave without transferring), **Data & privacy** (KUBICU emails — security, receipts always sent; data export ready to download for 7 days; account deletion lists owned workspaces, needs "DELETE", 14-day grace with cancel). Saving the name or confirming a new email updates the Owner's member record. Sign out returns to sign-in. Stored device-wide (`kubicu.account`). | Proposed | 14-day grace and 7-day export window are example figures. Real credentials, codes and messages need the auth backend. |
| D79 | Forms — paid add-on | Installed from **Extensions → Forms** (KUBICU, Operations, Creator plan and above, example ₦2,500/month; permissions screen; pause and uninstall). Until installed or while paused, **/app/forms** shows an explainer that links to the extension. **Forms list:** every form with status (Draft / Live / Closed / Closed automatically), field count, responses and unread count; "New form" from six starters (Quote request, RSVP, Customer survey, Order intake, Client onboarding, Job application) or blank. **Builder:** field list with move up/down (announced) and a field-settings panel; 17 field types in four groups (Text, Choice, Details, Special) including ₦ amount, rating, file, address, hidden attribution (source / campaign / page — never shown to visitors) and marketing consent (always optional, never pre-ticked); "Start a new step here" for multi-step forms with progress; "Show this question only when an earlier answer…" rules (is / is not / contains / is answered) shown as a sentence; one payment field per form, fixed or taken from an amount field (test mode). Set live is blocked until issues are fixed. **Responses:** Inbox / Unread / Archived, Mine, read/unread, archive, assign, CSV export; opening a response marks it read. **Settings:** intro, send button, thank-you message and Continue link, close date and maximum responses (closes itself), default assignee, spam (minimum fill time, per-visitor daily limit, hidden trap) and privacy note; duplicate, back to draft, delete with consequences. **Form block** (Conversion) picks a form; edit links go to Forms. Live submissions save to the inbox, add/update the contact in Audience (marketing consent only if the consent box was ticked), fire the "A form is submitted" automation trigger, appear in notifications ("New response to a form") and in Analytics → From visit to sale → Forms (saw / started / sent). Publish checks: add-on not installed, form deleted, draft, incomplete (errors), closed (warning). | Proposed | Price is an example. Files are never uploaded (file name only) and payments are test mode. The free Lead form and Newsletter blocks are unchanged. |
| D65 | Plan & billing (simulated) | Settings → Plan & billing: current plan (status, renewal, actions) leads; usage meters for products, campaigns/DealRooms, pages, seats, storage and AI credits (amber 80%, red 100%, upgrade button); test card on file; invoices (Paid/Failed/Refunded + Upcoming row) with printable view, example VAT 7.5% and a test-mode watermark; CSV export. Every upgrade button opens one plan-change flow: compare monthly/yearly (yearly = 10 months), **upgrades charge now** for the rest of the period less unused time; **downgrades wait for renewal**, listing exactly what goes over limits; nothing is ever deleted. Cancel = one reason + impact, ends at period end, resumable. Failed renewal → 7-day grace (app-wide banner, retry, update card) then Free. 14-day Pro trial once per workspace from Free, no card, reminder 3 days before, returns to Free. Test cards choose success / bank confirmation / declined / expired. Example prices inside the plan hypotheses (Creator ₦3,000, Pro ₦8,000, Business ₦20,000, Agency ₦50,000 per month); Enterprise = "Talk to us". Owner only (`billing.manage`); Admins read only. Controls: "Simulate next renewal" (succeeds/fails). | Proposed | Real provider, tax engine and legal invoice numbering are engineering/finance work. |
| D66 | Notifications centre | Bell in the sidebar header (collapsed: bottom rail) and phone top bar with unread count; inbox sheet (right on desktop, full-height on phones) grouped Needs action / Updates, filter by area, mark read / all read, each item links to the exact screen. Sources are existing events (orders, disputes, stock, bookings and cancellations, leads, follow-ups due, review requests, team notices, plan payment failed / renewal soon / trial ending, AI credits low, domain live or problem, DealRoom goal met/missed, campaign live/ended). Role-aware: you only receive what your role can act on. Settings → Notifications: event × channel (In app, Email, WhatsApp — the last two "preview, not sent"), daily digest and quiet hours, per person. Overview "Needs attention" gains "See all". | Proposed | Email/WhatsApp delivery needs a messaging provider. |
| D70 | Creators sell reviewed templates | Templates → **My templates** (Owner/Admin): "Sell a template" picks a space, name, category, region, price (free or ₦2,000–₦20,000), description and licence (own workspace / own + client workspaces); then a side-by-side check of what buyers get — name, tagline, profile photo, location, prices, dates, contacts, links, quotes and links to products/services/plans/discounts are removed; the creator confirms remaining photos are theirs and anyone shown agreed. Every submission and update goes to **Super admin → Modules & marketplace** review (4-point checklist required to approve; asking for changes or not approving needs a note the creator sees). Listed templates appear in Browse with "By {creator}", rating and reviews, and a Made by filter. Buying works like KUBICU premium templates; the sale records the version bought, so buyers keep it when the creator updates (an update keeps the old version on sale until approved). Refund within 7 days removes the licence and reverses the creator's share. Earnings: 70% creator / 30% KUBICU, pending for 7 days, then available; payout to a saved bank account from ₦5,000 (test mode). Creators can unlist and relist; staff can unlist. Review outcomes appear in notifications. Listings, sales and payouts live in one browser-wide store so workspaces on this device share the marketplace. | Proposed | Split, price range, refund window and payout minimum are example figures. Creator licence agreement needs legal review. Real payouts need a provider with split payments. |
| D72 | QR codes reach everything, and never dead-end | Codes and campaign links can open a space, page, the shop, a product, or straight onto an Event, Booking or Subscription block (the page scrolls to it and marks it; a linked Booking block opens its booking sheet). "Get QR code" shortcuts: My Spaces menu (main page, campaigns, DealRooms), the publish success screen, the product editor, Bookings → Services, and the block inspector for events, bookings and plans. If a destination is removed, scans fall back — removed block → its page; removed page/product/archived space → that space's home → the main page — and the owner gets an action notification and a warning on the QR card until it's re-pointed. | Proposed | Native ticketing now exists (D73); door check-in comes with Pass B. |
| D73a | 10-minute seat hold and Tickets funnel | Proposed | Pressing Checkout with tickets in the cart reserves those seats for 10 minutes (example figure) under a random per-tab id (no personal data). Other visitors see held seats as taken (“The last seats are held by people checking out — try again in a few minutes”); the buyer’s own hold counts as theirs. Going back and forth keeps the original expiry. A “Your seats are held 9:41” timer shows on every step after the cart and turns emphatic in the last minute; at zero the buyer returns to the cart with an explanation. Paying releases the hold; payment is still re-checked as the final guard. The guest list shows “+N held in checkout”. Analytics gains a **Tickets** funnel (Chose tickets → Started checkout → Got tickets) with ticket revenue, orders with tickets and the most chosen event; the demo workspace’s sample history includes ticket events. |
| D75 | Layout rules and shared layout primitives | Proposed | All app screens group content with five primitives in `components/ui/layout/`: **Section** (titled group, no chrome — the default), **Card** (the only bordered surface; 20px inset, 24px from md; optional icon, one header link via **CardLink**, `flush` body with a hairline for lists; `primary` emphasis adds a soft shadow and is used once per screen), **ListRow/List** (56px minimum rows, lead · title + one-line meta · trailing, hairline dividers, shared inset with the card), **Stat/StatGroup** (label above value, tabular numbers, one primary figure per group) and **DetailList/DetailSection** (label/value pairs on a fixed 144px label column for sheets and detail panels). Spacing: 32px from the page header to content and between page blocks (**PageStack**), 24px between cards in a grid, 16px heading → content, 24px + hairline between groups inside a sheet. Public pages and the canvas are out of scope. Commerce Overview is the reference screen; remaining screens move over pass by pass. |
| D74 | QR tickets, guest list and door check-in | Every seat gets its own ticket page (`/p/{handle}/ticket/{id}`, linked from the order page): a QR that encodes `KUBICU-TICKET:{code}`, the short code, guest name, ticket type, date, venue with an Open in Maps link, Add to calendar (.ics), copy link and WhatsApp share; refunded, cancelled or unpaid tickets show a plain state instead of the QR. Commerce → Events → Guest list shows every ticket with status (Not checked in / Checked in with time and who / Payment pending / Refunded), search, status and type filters, CSV export, check in or undo, resend (preview) and “Add guest” complimentary tickets (a ₦0 order so seats stay accurate). The check-in screen is full-screen and phone-first: camera scanning via the browser’s BarcodeDetector with jsQR (no further dependencies) as fallback, search by name, phone or code, a live “N / total in” counter, and a 2-second result card — Checked in (with Undo), Already checked in (time and by whom), Not valid — refunded, Not paid yet, Wrong event, or Not a ticket we know. Blocked or missing cameras fall back to search, and a prototype “simulate a scan” picker tests every result. New permission “Check in guests” for Owner, Admin and Sales. | Proposed | Check-ins are saved on this device only, so two doors on different phones won’t see each other’s check-ins until there’s a server. Resend is a preview. |
| D76 | Accessibility baseline (Batch 7) | Standard is WCAG 2.1 AA plus 2.5.7 and 2.5.8 from 2.2. Shared fixes: one focus trap for Dialog, Sheet and visitor overlays (focus in, Tab wraps, Escape closes, focus returns); app-wide reduced motion; Skip to content in app, canvas, Super admin and multi-page public sites; shared `announce()` live regions; dropdown error wiring; tooltips announced; 24px table checkboxes; Tabs/Menu Home/End. Public: seller colours are shifted to readable shades on live pages instead of blocking publish (saved theme untouched); one "Keep my seats" 10-minute extension per checkout (example figure); page language setting; h1 on standalone order/booking/download/subscription pages; step headings take focus; "Decorative only" images; forms name and focus the failing field. Canvas: moves announced, focus managed after delete/undo, Delete only from the selected block or its layer row, Keyboard shortcuts dialog (⌘/). Rules published on the design system page; findings in docs/ACCESSIBILITY_REPORT.md. | Proposed | Code review only — no automated scanner or real screen reader yet; the report lists open items and a manual checklist. |
| D75b | Super-admin operations | Working staff screens replace six placeholders: **Users & tenants** (every workspace with plan, status, spaces; change plan as an entitlement override, suspend/restore), **Feature flags** (kill switch + plan targeting that really gate AI page setup in the canvas and onboarding, and the storefront on public pages), **Moderation & trust** (reports from a new “Report this page” link on every public page; dismiss or take down — deciding one report closes every open report for that page; restore after appeal), **Support & announcements** (requests from a new sidebar Help menu with replies and staff-only notes; announcements shown as a dismissible banner by audience), **Audit log** (every privileged action with actor and required reason, filter and export, read-only) and **Security & health** (queues needing staff, illustrative service statuses, browser storage used). Every privileged action asks for a reason (≥10 characters). Suspension hides a workspace’s public pages (“temporarily unavailable”) and shows the owner a banner; takedowns show “This page was removed”. Owners’ draft previews still work. | Proposed | Stored in this browser; no staff roles/MFA, dual approval or appeals workflow yet. Pages & domains, Plans & entitlements, Payments, Orders & disputes, AI operations, Business reporting, Integrations, Data requests and Localisation remain planned screens. Help menu is in the desktop sidebar only. |
| D73 | Event ticketing through Commerce | Commerce → Events owns each event: name, cover, start/end with time zone, venue or online, overall capacity, refund policy, status (Draft / On sale / Paused / Cancelled) and up to 8 ticket types (price or free, quantity, sales open/close, min/max per order, note). Each event keeps a hidden "ticket" product whose variants are its ticket types and whose stock is seats left, so cart, checkout, discounts, Orders, refunds and Reports work unchanged. Seats, sales windows, per-order limits and capacity are checked in the cart and again at payment, so an event never oversells. The Event block can read from a Commerce event (live seats, "Only N left", "On sale 1 Dec", sold out, ended) and sells through checkout; free events skip the test card. Each seat is issued a ticket code with an optional guest name; full refunds and cancellations void tickets and free their seats; "Cancel event" refunds every ticket order in full. Paid tickets need Pro; free registration is on every plan. Ticket products are excluded from the shop, product pickers and the Free product limit. | Proposed | Example figures: 8 ticket types, 20 per order. The planned 10-minute seat hold is not built — seats are protected by the re-check at payment instead. QR ticket pages, guest list and check-in are Pass B. Refund wording needs legal review. |
| D71a | Staff console kept out of the customer app | The "Super admin" link is removed from the workspace sidebar and phone menu; the staff console is reached from "KUBICU team? Staff console" on the sign-in page (in production: separate staff sign-in). Its exit reads "Open customer app". Platform-set values shown to workspaces (e.g. visit retention, the visitor privacy notice) are labelled as set by KUBICU. | Proposed | — |
| D69 | Customer subscriptions (memberships + product boxes) | Commerce → Subscriptions: plans are a **Membership** (access/service, no order) or a **Product box** (catalogue items; each paid cycle creates a real order and takes stock — if the box can’t be filled the cycle is skipped, not charged). Plans: price per week/month/year, optional free trial (≤90 days), one-off setup fee, optional member cap, benefits, and subscriber rules (pause up to N cycles, skip, switch plan, cancel any time or with N days’ notice). Archive stops new sign-ups; current subscribers carry on. Subscribers list with status (Trial, Active, Paused, Payment failed, Cancelling, Ended), MRR / joined / left / failing totals, detail sheet (timeline, payments, pause, skip, free cycle, refund last payment, cancel now or at period end, copy portal link). **Subscription block** shows 1–3 plans; sign-up sheet = details → delivery (boxes) → review with plain renewal terms and an explicit “I agree to be charged … until I cancel” tick → test card (works / bank confirmation / declined). **Subscriber portal** at `/p/{handle}/subscription/{token}`: next charge, payments, pause, skip, change plan (difference shown, applies at next renewal), update card (settles a failed payment immediately), cancel (end of paid period; resume before then). Failed renewals retry on days 1, 3, 5 with a 7-day grace period, then end. Renewals run while KUBICU is open (“Run renewals now” on the Subscriptions screen; outcome follows the Simulate next renewal control). Pro plan and above. Also: Reports recurring revenue, notifications (new / failed / cancelled), automation triggers, Analytics subscription funnel, publish checks (no plans, removed/archived plans, unfillable boxes, plan gate). Sample plans and four sample subscribers for the demo workspace. | Proposed | Example figures: retry days, grace period, Pro gate. Real recurring card charges need a provider supporting stored cards/mandates (D40). Renewal wording needs legal review. Members-only content not included. |
| D68 | Subscription history in Plan & billing | Every subscription change is recorded with date, time and who did it ("Automatic" for renewals, retries and grace-period moves): trial started/ended, upgrades and cycle switches (with the charge and unused-time credit), scheduled downgrades and when they applied, cancel with reason and resume, renewal paid/failed, grace period started, retry succeeded/failed, moved to Free, card added/replaced/removed, one-off purchases. Entries link to their invoice. Filters (All / Plan / Payments / Card), CSV export, and a "What's coming" line above the list. The demo workspace's sample invoices get a matching sample history. | Proposed | Stored in the browser with the rest of billing; capped at 300 entries. |
| D67 | Extension and template marketplaces | **Extensions** (`/app/extensions`, curated only — built by KUBICU or a reviewed partner): staff picks, search, categories (Selling, Marketing, Analytics, Operations), Installed tab; detail sheet with what it adds, permissions, plan, price, versions (roll back to the previous one) and moderated reviews; install = permissions screen → test-card payment for paid add-ons (on invoices) → setup fields → done; pause, settings, uninstall (states what stays). Eight starters that genuinely change the app: Abandoned-cart recovery (adds a draft automation), Meta Pixel / Google tag (cookie notice on public pages; tags load only after Accept), Lagos delivery partners (adds three zones; uninstall removes exactly those), Order invoices (Invoice button on orders), plus previews using real data — Review requests, WhatsApp catalogue sync, Bank transfer (details saved). New permission "Install and manage extensions" (Owner, Admin). **Templates** (`/app/templates` + canvas panel + public page): filters by business type, region, colour mood and price; phone/desktop preview; "Apply to my main page" (snapshot, undoable, keeps your identity) or "Use on a new campaign"; premium templates bought once per workspace through the test checkout (example prices ₦3,500–₦5,000, on invoices); saved themes and sections shared privately by share code and imported into another workspace's library. | Proposed | Open developer submissions, creator-published templates and payouts are out of scope. |
| D64 | IntentFlow — blocks by visitor source | Per block, Visibility → "Show by visitor source": Everyone / Only / All except, chosen from WhatsApp, Instagram, TikTok, Facebook, X, Google, QR code, Campaign link, Direct, Other; a goal (taps, orders, enquiries, bookings); pause. Source is the visit's first source (`?src=`, utm, campaign link, referrer). Never personal traits. Safeguards: every page must keep ≥1 block everyone sees (publish blocked otherwise); an "except" rule covering every source is blocked. Canvas "Preview as" bar (only when rules exist) and draft preview `?as=`. IntentFlow screen lists every rule with live status and results vs. everyone else ("Too early to tell" below 30 visits each side, "pattern, not proof"); Pause / Resume / Roll back apply to the live page instantly without a publish, with Undo. Available on every plan (example). | Proposed | Real product evaluates the source server-side so the first paint is already correct. |
| D63a | Gradients mark AI | The product avoids gradients; AI is the single exception. `.ai-fill` (violet-700 → pink-700 → orange-700, white text ≥4.5:1), `.ai-soft` tint and `.ai-frame` hairline are used only on AI actions (`Button variant="ai"`), the "AI preview" tag, AI cards, drafts and suggestion output, so AI-made content is always recognisable. | Proposed | — |
| D63 | AI page setup and block suggestions (simulated) | Rule-based "AI preview", no model or API key. Page setup: brief → ≤4 questions → draft → review (before/after, assumptions, accept whole or block by block, or discard) → one undoable change. Layout comes from the closest starter; words only from the owner. Never generated: prices, reviews/testimonials, qualifications, delivery promises, refund or other policies — these become `[Add …]` placeholders that block publishing, or blocks hidden until filled. Block suggestions: heading, button label, product description from real Commerce data, FAQ answers from page facts only. Monthly credits per plan (example figures) with limit and failure states; failures use no credits. | Proposed | Real model, prompt safety review and pricing of credits are engineering/commercial work. |
| D49 | Mobile patterns for business screens | Below 768px: tables become card lists; side panels open full-screen with a pinned action bar; calendars become day agendas; kanban becomes tabs; inline tables of inputs stack with visible labels; tap targets ≥44px; main page action full-width. Delivered through shared components (Sheet, DataTable, SubNav, PageHeader) so new screens inherit them. | Proposed | Inventory adjustments keep a reason step on mobile. |
| D43 | Bookings model | Services + weekly hours with date changes, 30-min slot grid, break after each booking, minimum notice, daily cap. Times shown in the business zone with the visitor’s local time alongside. | Proposed | Pro plan. |
| D44 | Booking cancellation | Free changes until N hours before (default 24); later customer cancellations keep the deposit; owner cancellations always refund. Policy shown before booking. | Proposed | Legal review. |
| D45 | Consent | Three states: subscribed, service messages only, unsubscribed. Buying, booking, importing or being added never grants marketing consent. Owners can record an opt-out but never re-subscribe someone. | **Confirmed** | NDPA review of wording. |
| D46 | Automations | Linear readable steps; test runs on sample events; workspace-wide pause; marketing recipes include the opted-in condition. No real sending in prototype. | Proposed | |
| D19 | Undo model | Full history (100 steps), typing coalesced per field (~0.9s). Delete toast restores the block to its exact position. | Proposed | |
| D20 | Mobile editor model | Full-screen artboard, bottom tabs (Blocks, Design, Layers, Settings), tap-to-edit full-height sheet, up/down + grip reorder. | Proposed | Needs observed prototype tests (backlog Phase 0: 8–12 sessions). |

## Known conflicts recorded
- **Backlog "no mock screens / hard-coded data" vs. a front-end-only prototype.** Resolution: every interaction that can work in the browser does; sample data is labelled; unbuilt modules show honest "Planned" screens; nothing pretends to be connected.
- **Blueprint §5.2 sections vs. Pass 1 flat list.** See D7.
